**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `duel.push_of(mine, theirs, beat, role, tuning, battle, luck, foe_role="")`、`duel.harm_of(hp_cap, mine, theirs, beat, tuning)`、`duel.gamble_roll(rate, hp_cap, power, role, tuning, battle, rng)`、`duel.verdict_at(edge, hp, round_, rounds, tuning)`；`seal_duel` 的 `SIDES`、`CALL`／`CALL_QA`、`TextRequest`、`side_of`、`side_from_participant`、`side_from`、`open_duel`、`present`、`note_seen`、`proxy_weights`、`proxy`、`open_round`、`submit_problem`、`submit`、`sent`、`due`、`edge_words`、`resolve`、`verdict`、`take_chance`、`finish`、`advance`、`needs_write`、`card`、`foe_line`、`scene` 與字句常數。

設計 5.1 的 `push_of(…, rng)` 改成收已經擲好的 `luck`：純函式不收亂數，頭目戰照舊用 `Game.rng` 擲、奪璽單挑用 `seal.dice` 擲，取數的次數與順序跟原本一模一樣。

- [ ] **Step 1: 寫失敗的測試**

Create `tests/test_seal_duel.py`：

```python
"""奪璽單挑的純函式（玉璽碎片設計第五節；計畫 docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-12-玉璽-1-核心.md Task 7）。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import random

import pytest

from test_seal import SALT, put_shards
from tianxia import duel as duel_rules
from tianxia import seal, seal_duel
from tianxia.models import BattleTuning, DuelTuning, SealTuning
from tianxia.state import WorldState
from tianxia.world_state import SealDuel, SealSide, SharedWorldState


def _side(name, power=80.0, scores=None, role="", seen=None):
    return SealSide(name=name, power=power, scores=scores or {"強攻": 60.0, "固守": 60.0, "奇襲": 60.0}, role=role,
                    hp=300.0, hp_cap=300.0, last_seen=seen)


def _duel(cap=0.25, **over):
    d = SealDuel(id="7:chishou", shard="chishou", record=7, where="長社火攻", phase="active", starts_real=0.0,
                 challenger=_side("挑戰者"), holder=_side("懷璽"), cap=cap)
    return d.model_copy(update=over)


def _shared(content, duel=None, holder="懷璽"):
    st = SharedWorldState(season=WorldState(time=100.0), season_number=3, tianji=2)
    reg = seal.filled(st.seal, content)
    reg.salt = SALT
    reg.shards["chishou"].state, reg.shards["chishou"].holder, reg.shards["chishou"].since_season = "held", holder, 1
    if duel is not None:
        st.seal_duels = [duel]
    return st


def test_the_pure_pieces_draw_exactly_one_number_each():
    """duel.py 抽出來的純函式：gamble_roll 只取一次亂數、push_of 不取（luck 是呼叫端擲好的）；軍師 +10。
    頭目戰（exchange、gamble、verdict）照舊——tests/test_duel.py 一個字都不改照樣全過，是主要的保證。"""
    tuning, battle = DuelTuning(), BattleTuning()
    rng, ref = random.Random(5), random.Random(5)
    duel_rules.gamble_roll(50, 300.0, 80.0, "wis", tuning, battle, rng)
    ref.random()
    assert rng.getstate() == ref.getstate()
    won, delta, hp, rate = duel_rules.gamble_roll(50, 300.0, 80.0, "wis", tuning, battle, random.Random(1))
    assert rate == 60
    assert hp == (round(300 * tuning.success_hp) if won else round(300 * (tuning.fail_hp_base + 40 * tuning.fail_hp_per_risk)))
    assert duel_rules.push_of(60.0, 60.0, 0, "", tuning, battle, 2.6) == 3  # 等份量、不剋：只剩運氣
    assert duel_rules.verdict_at(91.0, 10.0, 1, 5, tuning) == "大勝" and duel_rules.verdict_at(50.0, 0.0, 1, 5, tuning) == "落敗"


def test_both_sides_lose_their_own_pool_and_a_lore_side_takes_half_the_counter():
    """5.2：兩邊各扣自己的池子；被剋的一方是參謀時剋制那一截減半，剋人的一方遇上參謀時推力的那一截也減半。"""
    tuning, battle = DuelTuning(), BattleTuning()
    assert duel_rules.push_of(60.0, 60.0, -1, "", tuning, battle, 0.0) == -8
    assert duel_rules.push_of(60.0, 60.0, -1, "lore", tuning, battle, 0.0) == -4
    assert duel_rules.push_of(60.0, 60.0, 1, "", tuning, battle, 0.0, foe_role="lore") == 4
    assert duel_rules.harm_of(300.0, 60.0, 60.0, -1, tuning) == round(300 * 0.08 * 1.6)
    assert duel_rules.harm_of(300.0, 60.0, 60.0, 1, tuning) == round(300 * 0.08 * 0.5)


def test_a_round_with_one_gamble_ignores_the_other_move(content):
    """只有一邊放手一搏：搏的那一邊照 gamble_roll 擲，另一邊這一招不算、不扣血（同頭目戰「你搶了先手，或自己栽了」）。"""
    put_shards(content)
    d = _duel()
    d.challenger.present = True
    seal_duel.submit(d, "challenger", text="點燃糧車", rate=100, stories=("挑戰者點燃糧車，火光沖天。", "挑戰者被火燎了眉毛。"))
    d.proxy_move["holder"] = "強攻"
    line = seal_duel.resolve(d, content, SALT, 2)
    assert d.holder.hp == 300.0 and d.edge > 50 and d.challenger.gambles == 1
    assert "挑戰者點燃糧車" in line and line.startswith("奪璽第 1 回合")


def test_both_gambles_add_up(content):
    put_shards(content)
    d = _duel()
    for who in seal_duel.SIDES:
        getattr(d, who).present = True
        seal_duel.submit(d, who, text="拚了", rate=100)
    seal_duel.resolve(d, content, SALT, 2)
    assert d.edge == 50.0  # 兩邊同強、都成：推力抵銷
    assert d.challenger.gambles == d.holder.gambles == 1


def test_the_verdict_from_the_challenger_side():
    tuning = DuelTuning()
    d = _duel()
    d.holder.hp = 0
    assert seal_duel.verdict(d, tuning) == "大勝"  # 持有者池子見底（不算提前大勝）
    d.challenger.hp = 0
    assert seal_duel.verdict(d, tuning) == "落敗"  # 同一回合兩邊見底：偏持有者
    assert seal_duel.verdict(_duel(edge=92.0), tuning) == "提前大勝"
    assert seal_duel.verdict(_duel(edge=60.0, round=5), tuning) == "險勝"
    assert seal_duel.verdict(_duel(edge=60.0, round=3), tuning) is None


def test_the_proxy_leans_on_the_strongest_move():
    """5.6：招的權重是（份量 ÷ 最高）^4；75／60／90 的人最常出的那一招約 59%。"""
    weights = seal_duel.proxy_weights(_side("某", scores={"強攻": 75.0, "固守": 60.0, "奇襲": 90.0}), SealTuning())
    assert weights[2] / sum(weights) == pytest.approx(0.595, abs=0.01)
    assert seal_duel.proxy_weights(_side("空", scores={"強攻": 0.0}), SealTuning()) == [1.0, 1.0, 1.0]


def test_only_the_absent_side_gets_a_tell_and_cannot_send(content):
    """回合一開：45 秒內推過這場的人才在場；不在場的那一邊預擲一招與一句架勢（只給對手看），這一回合送不出去。"""
    put_shards(content)
    d = _duel()
    d.challenger.last_seen = 970.0
    seal_duel.open_round(d, 1000.0, content, SALT, 2)
    assert d.challenger.present and not d.holder.present
    assert "holder" in d.tell and "challenger" not in d.tell and d.proxy_move["holder"] in ("強攻", "固守", "奇襲")
    assert seal_duel.submit_problem(d, "holder", content.config.seal) == seal_duel.LATE
    assert seal_duel.submit_problem(d, "challenger", content.config.seal) is None
    assert seal_duel.sent(d) == 0  # 代打那一手到結算才算


def test_the_round_waits_at_least_the_pace_and_at_most_the_round_seconds(content):
    put_shards(content)
    cfg = content.config.seal
    d = _duel()
    seal_duel.open_round(d, 1000.0, content, SALT, 2)  # 兩邊都不在場
    assert not seal_duel.due(d, 1019.0, cfg) and seal_duel.due(d, 1020.0, cfg)
    d.challenger.last_seen = 1000.0
    seal_duel.open_round(d, 1000.0, content, SALT, 2)
    assert not seal_duel.due(d, 1060.0, cfg)  # 在場的還沒送：等
    seal_duel.submit(d, "challenger", move="強攻")
    assert not seal_duel.due(d, 1010.0, cfg) and seal_duel.due(d, 1020.0, cfg)  # 送了也等滿 20 秒（節奏看不出誰不在場）
    d.challenger.last_seen = 1990.0
    seal_duel.open_round(d, 2000.0, content, SALT, 2)
    assert not seal_duel.due(d, 2100.0, cfg) and seal_duel.due(d, 2120.0, cfg)  # 在場卻一直沒送：120 秒照樣結算


def test_a_side_may_gamble_at_most_twice(content):
    put_shards(content)
    d = _duel()
    d.challenger.present, d.challenger.gambles = True, 2
    assert seal_duel.submit_problem(d, "challenger", content.config.seal, text=True) == seal_duel.NO_MORE_GAMBLE
    assert seal_duel.submit_problem(d, "challenger", content.config.seal, text=False) is None


@pytest.mark.parametrize("seed", range(30))
def test_the_take_chance_never_passes_the_duel_cap(content, seed):
    """5.8 的性質測試：任意出招、任意成功率、雙方在不在場，易手機率 ≤ cap（＝25% × 局勢 × 護璽）。"""
    put_shards(content)
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    cap = 0.25 * rng.choice([1.0, 0.7])
    d = _duel(cap=cap)
    result = None
    for n in range(5):
        seal_duel.open_round(d, n * 200.0, content, SALT, seed)
        for who in seal_duel.SIDES:
            if rng.random() < 0.5:
                getattr(d, who).present = True
                if rng.random() < 0.5 and getattr(d, who).gambles < 2:
                    seal_duel.submit(d, who, text="亂來", rate=rng.randint(0, 100))
                else:
                    seal_duel.submit(d, who, move=rng.choice(["強攻", "固守", "奇襲"]))
        seal_duel.resolve(d, content, SALT, seed)
        result = seal_duel.verdict(d, content.config.duel)
        if result is not None:
            break
    assert seal_duel.take_chance(d, result or "僵持", content.config.seal) <= cap + 1e-9


def test_a_duel_nobody_attends_finishes_in_paced_rounds_and_is_the_same_every_time(content):
    """兩邊都不在場：每回合 20 秒；最多五回合收場、寫天下大事；同樣的種子結果一模一樣（誰觸發都一樣）。"""
    put_shards(content)

    def run():
        st = _shared(content, _duel(phase="muster", starts_real=0.0))
        lines, t = [], 0.0
        while st.seal_duels and t < 400:
            lines += seal_duel.advance(st, content, t)
            t += 20.0
        return lines, st.seal.history[-1].model_dump(), st.seal.shards["chishou"].holder

    first = run()
    assert first == run()
    _, event, holder = first
    assert event["kind"] in ("take", "keep") and 1 <= len(event["lines"]) <= 5
    assert holder == ("挑戰者" if event["kind"] == "take" else "懷璽")


def test_finish_checks_the_registry_before_handing_over(content):
    """5.9：持有者還是原來那人、挑戰者此刻手上沒有碎片才換；不成立記成沒打成（unsettled）。"""
    put_shards(content)
    d = _duel(fixed=True)
    st = _shared(content, d)
    st.seal.shards["xuanniu"].state, st.seal.shards["xuanniu"].holder = "held", "挑戰者"  # 他排隊的時候拿到了一片
    event = seal_duel.finish(st, d, content, "提前大勝", 500.0)
    assert event.kind == "unsettled" and st.seal.shards["chishou"].holder == "懷璽" and st.seal_duels == []
    d2 = _duel(fixed=True)
    st2 = _shared(content, d2)
    event2 = seal_duel.finish(st2, d2, content, "提前大勝", 500.0)
    shard = st2.seal.shards["chishou"]
    assert event2.kind == "take" and (shard.holder, shard.former, shard.since_season) == ("挑戰者", "懷璽", 3)


def test_the_card_shows_the_tell_only_to_the_opponent_and_flips_the_edge_for_the_holder(content):
    put_shards(content)
    d = _duel(edge=70.0)
    d.tell = {"holder": "他退了半步，門戶守得嚴嚴實實。"}
    as_challenger = seal_duel.card(d, "挑戰者", 1000.0, content)
    as_holder = seal_duel.card(d, "懷璽", 1000.0, content)
    as_crowd = seal_duel.card(d, "路人", 1000.0, content)
    assert as_challenger["tell"] and not as_holder["tell"] and not as_crowd["tell"]
    assert "你" in as_holder["edge"] and as_holder["edge"] != as_challenger["edge"]
    assert (as_crowd["challenger"], as_crowd["holder"], as_crowd["shard"]) == ("挑戰者", "懷璽", "螭首")
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 寫程式**

`tianxia/duel.py`：`counter` 之後加四個純函式，原本的 `exchange`、`gamble`、`verdict` 改成包一層（亂數取用的次數與順序一模一樣）：

```python
def push_of(
    mine: float, theirs: float, beat: int, role: str, tuning: DuelTuning, battle: BattleTuning, luck: float, foe_role: str = "",
) -> int:
    """一招的推力（往「我」算）：push_base ×（2 × 我 ÷ 兩邊 − 1）＋ counter_push × 剋 ＋ 運氣。我被剋而且我是參謀、或我剋人而對方是
    參謀時，剋的那一截減半（role_counter_relief）。luck 是呼叫端擲好的（頭目戰照舊用 Game.rng，奪璽單挑用 seal.dice）。"""
    ratio = mine / (mine + theirs) if mine + theirs > 0 else 0.5
    relief = 1 - battle.role_counter_relief
    weight = beat * relief if (beat < 0 and role == "lore") or (beat > 0 and foe_role == "lore") else beat
    return round(tuning.push_base * (2 * ratio - 1) + tuning.counter_push * weight + luck)


def harm_of(hp_cap: float, mine: float, theirs: float, beat: int, tuning: DuelTuning) -> int:
    """我這一招挨的打（還沒跟剩下的池子取小）：上限 × hit × 剋制倍數（被剋 hit_countered、剋人 hit_countering）× √(對方 ÷ 我)（夾住）。"""
    harm = tuning.hit_countered if beat < 0 else tuning.hit_countering if beat > 0 else 1.0
    scale = max(0.5, min(tuning.hit_scale_max, (theirs / mine) ** 0.5)) if mine > 0 else tuning.hit_scale_max
    return round(hp_cap * tuning.hit * harm * scale)


def gamble_roll(
    rate: int, hp_cap: float, power: float, role: str, tuning: DuelTuning, battle: BattleTuning, rng: random.Random,
) -> tuple[bool, int, int, int]:
    """放手一搏擲一次：（成了沒有, 推力〔還沒夾〕, 扣多少池子〔還沒跟剩下的取小〕, 實際的成功率）。軍師 +role_gamble_rate；
    成了乘實力倍數，沒成的不乘。亂數取一次。"""
    rate = max(0, min(100, rate + (battle.role_gamble_rate if role == "wis" else 0)))
    risk = 100 - rate
    if rng.random() * 100 < rate:
        might = battle_instance.strength(battle, power) / battle.gamble_strength_ref
        might = max(battle.gamble_strength_min, min(battle.gamble_strength_max, might))
        return True, max(1, round((tuning.gamble_base + risk * tuning.gamble_per_risk) * might)), round(hp_cap * tuning.success_hp), rate
    delta = -min(round(tuning.fail_cap), round(risk * tuning.fail_per_risk))
    return False, delta, round(hp_cap * (tuning.fail_hp_base + risk * tuning.fail_hp_per_risk)), rate


def verdict_at(edge: float, hp: float, round_: int, rounds: int, tuning: DuelTuning) -> str | None:
    """verdict 的本體：池子見底或氣勢到 early_lose 以下提前落敗、到 early_win 以上提前大勝；打滿 rounds 回合看氣勢。還沒是 None。"""
    if hp <= 0 or edge <= tuning.early_lose:
        return "落敗"
    if edge >= tuning.early_win:
        return "大勝"
    if round_ < rounds:
        return None
    if edge >= tuning.big:
        return "大勝"
    if edge >= tuning.win:
        return "險勝"
    return "僵持" if edge > tuning.draw else "落敗"
```

- `exchange` 的本體換成（`ratio`、`weight`、`harm`、`scale` 那幾行拿掉）：

```python
    mine = duel.scores.get(move) or battle_instance.strength(battle, duel.power) * tuning.foe_fit
    theirs = foe_strength(battle, tuning, boss)
    beat = counter(move, duel.move)
    delta = push_of(mine, theirs, beat, duel.role, tuning, battle, rng.uniform(-tuning.luck, tuning.luck))
    hp = min(duel.hp, harm_of(duel.hp_cap, mine, theirs, beat, tuning))
    before = duel.edge
    duel.edge = _clamp(duel.edge + delta)
    duel.hp -= hp
    return round(duel.edge - before), hp, beat
```

- `gamble` 的本體換成：

```python
    won, delta, hp, rate = gamble_roll(rate, duel.hp_cap, duel.power, duel.role, tuning, battle, rng)
    hp = min(duel.hp, hp)
    before = duel.edge
    duel.edge = _clamp(duel.edge + delta)
    duel.hp -= hp
    return won, round(duel.edge - before), hp, rate
```

- `verdict` 換成 `return verdict_at(duel.edge, duel.hp, duel.round, boss.rounds, tuning)`。
- `edge_words(edge: float, viewer: str = "你")`：字裡的「你」換成 `{viewer}`（「兩邊僵持不下」照舊）；頭目戰的呼叫照舊不傳。

Create `tianxia/seal_duel.py`：

```python
"""奪璽單挑（玉璽碎片設計第五節；PM 2026-10-11 乙）：挑戰者對持有者，照單人頭目戰的規則對稱地打 rounds 回合（duel.py 的純函式）。

- 兩邊都用快照（SealSide，5.3）：份量（拿掉騎兵與部下，計畫「要 PM 定的」第 4 條）、職位、氣血池在開單挑時抓好；池子各扣各的，
  打完不扣回角色、不磨兵器、沒有獎勵（名望與碎片在補送時給）。
- 一回合（5.2）：兩邊都出招時推力＝duel.push_of（往挑戰者算），兩邊各扣自己的池子（duel.harm_of）；有人放手一搏時搏的那一邊照
  duel.gamble_roll 擲，另一邊這一招不算、不扣血；兩邊都搏推力相加。一場每一邊最多搏 gamble_cap 次。
- 在場（5.5）：回合開始時 last_seen 在 presence_window 秒內才算；不在場的那一邊回合一開就預擲一招與一句架勢（代打，5.6），架勢只給
  對手看；在場卻逾時沒送的那一手在結算時才代出（不給架勢）。判成不在場的那一邊這一回合送不出去（LATE）。
- 結算的時機（5.4）：最早在回合開始 pace_seconds 後；在場的都送了就結算，否則等到 round_seconds。
- 勝負（挑戰者的角度）：verdict；再擲（5.8）：take_chance＝cap × take_share[結果]，cap 開單挑時已經乘過局勢與護璽，四個都不超過 1。
- 亂數（5.13）：一回合兩份 seal-round|id|回合|邊（挑戰者那一份先取運氣，各自再取放手一搏的成敗）；代打 seal-proxy|id|回合|邊
  （先取招、再取架勢真假、再取架勢那一句）；再擲 seal-take|id（一次）。全不碰 Game.rng，誰觸發結算結果都一樣。
只改傳進來的 SealDuel 與 SharedWorldState（天下大事、江湖史、全服登記）；不碰任何人的存檔、不叫模型。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import random
from typing import NamedTuple

from . import battle_instance, seal
from . import duel as duel_rules
from .battle_instance import CAVALRY_MOVES, DEFAULT_FREE_TEXT_SUCCESS_RATE, BattleParticipant
from .models import MOVES, Content, DuelTuning, SealTuning
from .world_state import SealDuel, SealEvent, SealSide, SharedWorldState

SIDES = ("challenger", "holder")
TEXT = "text"  # pending 裡放手一搏的那一手
WIN_VERDICTS = ("提前大勝", "大勝", "險勝")  # 挑戰者打贏了單挑（PM 丁：再擲沒中名望 +1）

# 待 joy 潤
LATE = "這一回合來不及了，下一回合再出手。"
SENT = "已出招，等對方"
NO_MORE_GAMBLE = "這一場你已經放手一搏兩次了，只剩三招。"
GONE = "這一回合已經結算了，下一回合再出手。"
CALL = "{challenger}（此役戰功居前）向懷璽的{holder}下了戰書，一炷香後陣前單挑，爭『{shard}』。"
CALL_QA = "{challenger}向懷璽的{holder}下了戰書，一炷香後陣前單挑，爭『{shard}』。"  # 挑戰者不是參戰者（小伺服器補上的旁觀散人、管理者不掛決戰開的那一場）
ROUND = "奪璽第 {n} 回合：{lead}——{edge}。"
CLOSE_LINE = "最後氣勢越偏向挑戰者，碎片越可能易主；璽有靈，未必跟著贏家走。"
TAKE = "{challenger}在{where}陣前奪得{holder}懷中的『{shard}』，{holder}力戰失璽。"
NEAR = "{challenger}力挫{holder}，『{shard}』在兩人之間一閃，終究落回{holder}懷中，懷璽不失。"
KEEP = "{holder}擋下了{challenger}，懷璽不失。"
UNSETTLED = "{challenger}與{holder}爭『{shard}』的單挑沒有打完，碎片留在{holder}懷中。"
GAMBLE_WON, GAMBLE_LOST = "{name}放手一搏——成了！", "{name}放手一搏——沒成，反倒露了破綻。"
SETTING = "兩人陣前單挑、爭奪玉璽碎片的"  # 給 battle_instance.assess_gamble
PLACE = "單挑"


class TextRequest(NamedTuple):
    """放手一搏的 A 段開的單子（Game.seal_text_request → server.seal_text → Game.seal_gamble）。"""

    duel: str
    round: int
    side: str
    text: str
    name: str
    faction_name: str
    title: str
    scene: str
    target: str


def other(who: str) -> str:
    return "holder" if who == "challenger" else "challenger"


def side_of(duel: SealDuel, name: str) -> str | None:
    k = seal.key(name)
    return next((w for w in SIDES if seal.key(getattr(duel, w).name) == k), None)


def unmounted(p: BattleParticipant) -> dict[str, float]:
    """參戰者的份量拿掉騎兵與部下（強攻、奇襲除回去）：單挑照頭目戰（_battle_scores(mounted=False)）。"""
    ride = (1 + p.cavalry) * (1 + p.troop_bonus)
    return {m: round(v / ride, 1) if m in CAVALRY_MOVES and ride > 0 else v for m, v in p.scores.items()}


def side_from_participant(p: BattleParticipant, faction: str | None) -> SealSide:
    """參戰者的快照（5.3 前兩列）：威力、份量、職位、氣血上限；池子從滿血開始（決戰裡那一份池子不帶進來）。"""
    return SealSide(name=p.name, faction=faction, power=p.power, scores=unmounted(p), role=p.role, hp=p.neili_cap, hp_cap=p.neili_cap)


def side_from(name: str, faction: str | None, power: float, scores: dict[str, float], role: str, hp_cap: float, fresh: bool = False) -> SealSide:
    """沒參戰的那一邊（持有者、小伺服器補上的旁觀散人）：engine 照他的 Game 算好給（_battle_power、_battle_scores(mounted=False)…）。"""
    return SealSide(name=name, faction=faction, power=power, scores=dict(scores), role=role, hp=hp_cap, hp_cap=hp_cap, fresh=fresh)


def open_duel(
    duel_id: str, shard: str, record: int | None, event_id: str | None, where: str, tier: str, cap: float,
    challenger: SealSide, holder: SealSide, now: float, cfg: SealTuning,
) -> SealDuel:
    return SealDuel(
        id=duel_id, shard=shard, record=record, event_id=event_id, where=where, tier=tier, cap=cap, phase="muster",
        starts_real=now + cfg.muster_seconds, challenger=challenger, holder=holder, rounds=cfg.rounds,
    )


def present(side: SealSide, now: float, cfg: SealTuning) -> bool:
    return side.last_seen is not None and 0 <= now - side.last_seen <= cfg.presence_window


def note_seen(duel: SealDuel, who: str, now: float, cfg: SealTuning) -> bool:
    """他自己的請求推了這場單挑：記 last_seen；存著的比現在早 seen_write_seconds 以上才寫（低頻寫入，5.5）。回傳有沒有改。"""
    s = getattr(duel, who)
    if s.last_seen is not None and now - s.last_seen < cfg.seen_write_seconds:
        return False
    s.last_seen = now
    return True


def proxy_weights(side: SealSide, cfg: SealTuning) -> list[float]:
    """代打的招的權重：（這一招的份量 ÷ 三招最高）^proxy_power（5.6）；份量全是 0 就平均。"""
    values = [max(0.0, side.scores.get(m, 0.0)) for m in MOVES]
    top = max(values)
    return [(v / top) ** cfg.proxy_power for v in values] if top > 0 else [1.0] * len(MOVES)


def proxy(side: SealSide, rng: random.Random, cfg: SealTuning, tuning: DuelTuning) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """代打預擲一招與一句架勢（tell_truth 的機會照實，其餘是另一招的架勢）：先取招、再取真假、再取句子。"""
    move = rng.choices(MOVES, weights=proxy_weights(side, cfg))[0]
    shown = move if rng.random() < tuning.tell_truth else rng.choice([m for m in MOVES if m != move])
    return move, rng.choice(duel_rules.TELLS[shown])


def open_round(duel: SealDuel, now: float, content: Content, salt: str, tianji: int) -> None:
    """一回合開始：判兩邊在不在場；不在場的那一邊預擲一招與一句架勢（5.6）。"""
    cfg, tuning = content.config.seal, content.config.duel
    duel.opened_real = now
    duel.pending, duel.rates, duel.texts, duel.stories, duel.proxy_move, duel.tell = {}, {}, {}, {}, {}, {}
    for who in SIDES:
        s = getattr(duel, who)
        s.present = present(s, now, cfg)
        if not s.present:
            move, tell = proxy(s, seal.dice(salt, tianji, "seal-proxy", duel.id, duel.round + 1, who), cfg, tuning)
            duel.proxy_move[who], duel.tell[who] = move, tell
            s.proxied = True


def submit_problem(duel: SealDuel, who: str, cfg: SealTuning, text: bool = False) -> str | None:
    """這一邊這一回合送不送得出去；None＝可以。"""
    if duel.phase != "active":
        return GONE
    s = getattr(duel, who)
    if not s.present:
        return LATE
    if who in duel.pending:
        return SENT
    if text and s.gambles >= cfg.gamble_cap:
        return NO_MORE_GAMBLE
    return None


def submit(duel: SealDuel, who: str, move: str | None = None, text: str | None = None, rate: int | None = None,
           stories: tuple[str, str] | list[str] | None = None) -> None:
    """記這一邊這一回合的一手（呼叫端先看過 submit_problem）。送出不等於結算（同決戰）。"""
    if text is None:
        duel.pending[who] = move or "固守"
        return
    duel.pending[who], duel.texts[who] = TEXT, text
    duel.rates[who] = max(0, min(100, int(DEFAULT_FREE_TEXT_SUCCESS_RATE if rate is None else rate)))
    if stories:
        duel.stories[who] = [str(stories[0]), str(stories[1])]


def sent(duel: SealDuel) -> int:
    """「已出招 X／2」：只算親自送出的（代打那一手到結算才算，5.6）。"""
    return len(duel.pending)


def due(duel: SealDuel, now: float, cfg: SealTuning) -> bool:
    if duel.phase != "active" or duel.opened_real is None:
        return False
    waited = now - duel.opened_real
    if waited < cfg.pace_seconds:
        return False
    here = [w for w in SIDES if getattr(duel, w).present]
    return all(w in duel.pending for w in here) or waited >= cfg.round_seconds


def _weight(side: SealSide, move: str, content: Content) -> float:
    return side.scores.get(move) or battle_instance.strength(content.config.battle, side.power) * content.config.duel.foe_fit


def edge_words(duel: SealDuel, viewer: str | None = None) -> str:
    """氣勢那一句：viewer 是持有者就倒過來、寫「你」；挑戰者寫「你」；其他人（圍觀、天下大事）寫挑戰者的名號。"""
    who = side_of(duel, viewer) if viewer else None
    edge = 100 - duel.edge if who == "holder" else duel.edge
    return duel_rules.edge_words(edge, "你" if who else duel.challenger.name)


def resolve(duel: SealDuel, content: Content, salt: str, tianji: int) -> str:
    """結算這一回合（呼叫端先看過 due）：照 5.2 推氣勢、扣兩邊的池子，回這一回合那一句（兩邊具名，天下大事與戰報用）。"""
    cfg, tuning, battle_t = content.config.seal, content.config.duel, content.config.battle
    n = duel.round + 1
    c, h = duel.challenger, duel.holder
    rolls = {w: seal.dice(salt, tianji, "seal-round", duel.id, n, w) for w in SIDES}
    plays: dict[str, str] = {}
    for w in SIDES:
        if w in duel.pending:
            plays[w] = duel.pending[w]
        elif w in duel.proxy_move:
            plays[w] = duel.proxy_move[w]
        else:  # 在場卻逾時沒送：結算時才代出（不給架勢）
            plays[w] = proxy(getattr(duel, w), seal.dice(salt, tianji, "seal-proxy", duel.id, n, w), cfg, tuning)[0]
            getattr(duel, w).proxied = True
    luck = rolls["challenger"].uniform(-tuning.luck, tuning.luck)  # 先取運氣：不論有沒有人搏，取數順序固定
    gamblers = [w for w in SIDES if plays[w] == TEXT]
    harm = {w: 0 for w in SIDES}
    push = 0
    if not gamblers:
        mc, mh = plays["challenger"], plays["holder"]
        sc, sh = _weight(c, mc, content), _weight(h, mh, content)
        beat = duel_rules.counter(mc, mh)
        push = duel_rules.push_of(sc, sh, beat, c.role, tuning, battle_t, luck, foe_role=h.role)
        harm["challenger"] = duel_rules.harm_of(c.hp_cap, sc, sh, beat, tuning)
        harm["holder"] = duel_rules.harm_of(h.hp_cap, sh, sc, -beat, tuning)
        lead = f"{c.name}{mc}，{h.name}以{mh}相迎"
    else:
        parts = []
        for w in gamblers:
            s = getattr(duel, w)
            s.gambles += 1
            won, delta, hp, _ = duel_rules.gamble_roll(
                duel.rates.get(w, DEFAULT_FREE_TEXT_SUCCESS_RATE), s.hp_cap, s.power, s.role, tuning, battle_t, rolls[w],
            )
            push += delta if w == "challenger" else -delta
            harm[w] = hp
            story = (duel.stories.get(w) or ["", ""])[0 if won else 1]
            parts.append((story or (GAMBLE_WON if won else GAMBLE_LOST).format(name=s.name)).rstrip("。"))
        lead = "；".join(parts)
    c.hp = max(0.0, c.hp - harm["challenger"])
    h.hp = max(0.0, h.hp - harm["holder"])
    duel.edge = max(0.0, min(100.0, duel.edge + push))
    duel.round = n
    line = ROUND.format(n=n, lead=lead, edge=edge_words(duel))
    duel.lines.append(line)
    return line


def verdict(duel: SealDuel, tuning: DuelTuning) -> str | None:
    """分出勝負沒有（挑戰者的角度，5.2）：挑戰者池子見底或氣勢 ≤ early_lose 落敗（同一回合兩邊見底也算落敗，偏持有者）；
    持有者池子見底是大勝（不算提前大勝）；氣勢 ≥ early_win 提前大勝；打滿看 big／win／draw。還沒是 None。"""
    if duel.challenger.hp <= 0 or duel.edge <= tuning.early_lose:
        return "落敗"
    if duel.holder.hp <= 0:
        return "大勝"
    if duel.edge >= tuning.early_win:
        return "提前大勝"
    return duel_rules.verdict_at(duel.edge, duel.challenger.hp, duel.round, duel.rounds, tuning)


def take_chance(duel: SealDuel, result: str, cfg: SealTuning) -> float:
    """這一場易手的機率（5.8）：cap（已乘局勢與護璽）× 單挑係數，最多 Config.seal.cap。"""
    return min(cfg.cap, duel.cap * cfg.take_share.get(result, 0.0))


def _still_valid(shared: SharedWorldState, duel: SealDuel) -> bool:
    """持有者還是原來那人、挑戰者此刻手上沒有碎片（開打前、寫入前都重驗，5.9、5.12）。"""
    shard = shared.seal.shards.get(duel.shard)
    return (shard is not None and shard.state == "held" and seal.key(shard.holder) == seal.key(duel.holder.name)
            and seal.key(duel.challenger.name) not in seal.holders(shared.seal))


def finish(shared: SharedWorldState, duel: SealDuel, content: Content, result: str | None, now: float) -> SealEvent:
    """收場（5.9）：重驗之後再擲 seal-take|id（管理者定了結果就照它），易手或懷璽不失；寫登記一筆、天下大事、江湖史；這一場從清單
    拿掉，排隊的下一場從現在起下戰書。result 是 None（季終、管理者取消）或重驗不過：記成沒打成，只寫天下大事。"""
    cfg = content.config.seal
    reg = seal.filled(shared.seal, content)
    season = shared.season_number
    c, h = duel.challenger, duel.holder
    shard_name = content.seal[duel.shard].name if duel.shard in content.seal else duel.shard
    words = {"challenger": c.name, "holder": h.name, "shard": shard_name, "where": duel.where or "江湖"}
    common = dict(shard=duel.shard, record=duel.record, where=duel.where, lines=list(duel.lines), fresh=h.fresh,
                  proxied=[w for w in SIDES if getattr(duel, w).proxied])
    shared.seal_duels = [d for d in shared.seal_duels if d.id != duel.id]
    if shared.seal_duels:
        shared.seal_duels[0].starts_real = now + cfg.muster_seconds
    if result is None or not _still_valid(shared, duel):
        text = UNSETTLED.format(**words)
        seal.rumor(shared.season, text)
        return seal.log(reg, season, "unsettled", holder=h.name, other=c.name, text=text, **common)
    roll = seal.dice(reg.salt, shared.tianji, "seal-take", duel.id).random()
    taken = duel.fixed if duel.fixed is not None else roll < take_chance(duel, result, cfg)
    shard = reg.shards[duel.shard]
    if taken:
        shard.holder, shard.since_season, shard.former, shard.absent = c.name, season, h.name, 0
        text = TAKE.format(**words)
        event = seal.log(reg, season, "take", holder=c.name, other=h.name, result=result, text=text, **common)
    else:
        text = (NEAR if result in WIN_VERDICTS else KEEP).format(**words)
        event = seal.log(reg, season, "keep", holder=h.name, other=c.name, result=result, text=text, **common)
    seal.rumor(shared.season, text)
    seal.chronicle(shared.season, text)
    return event


def advance(shared: SharedWorldState, content: Content, now: float) -> list[str]:
    """把排第一的那一場推一步（_seal_tick、送出一手之後都叫），一次最多結算一回合：下戰書時間到了就開打（先重驗）；回合該結算就結算、
    分出勝負就收場，否則開下一回合。暫停中不推；季已經結束（自然收季、管理者收季）就記成沒打完（5.11）。回傳這一步寫的那幾句。"""
    if not shared.seal_duels or shared.paused_at is not None:
        return []
    duel = shared.seal_duels[0]
    reg = seal.filled(shared.seal, content)  # 密鹽只讀（伺服器啟動時產生；測試與腳本是空字串）
    if shared.season.ended:
        return [finish(shared, duel, content, None, now).text]
    if duel.phase == "muster":
        if now < duel.starts_real:
            return []
        if not _still_valid(shared, duel):
            return [finish(shared, duel, content, None, now).text]
        duel.phase = "active"
        open_round(duel, now, content, reg.salt, shared.tianji)
        return []
    if not due(duel, now, content.config.seal):
        return []
    line = resolve(duel, content, reg.salt, shared.tianji)
    seal.rumor(shared.season, line)
    result = verdict(duel, content.config.duel)
    if result is not None:
        return [line, finish(shared, duel, content, result, now).text]
    open_round(duel, now, content, reg.salt, shared.tianji)
    return [line]


def needs_write(duel: SealDuel, who: str | None, now: float, cfg: SealTuning, *, own: bool, ended: bool) -> bool:
    """_seal_tick 要不要開一次 world.mutate（每 10 秒的輪詢不能每次都寫）：季結束了、要記自己的 last_seen、下戰書到點、回合該結算。"""
    if ended:
        return True
    if own and who is not None:
        seen = getattr(duel, who).last_seen
        if seen is None or now - seen >= cfg.seen_write_seconds:
            return True
    return now >= duel.starts_real if duel.phase == "muster" else due(duel, now, cfg)


def card(duel: SealDuel, viewer: str | None, now: float, content: Content) -> dict:
    """江湖頁「奪璽」卡（5.10，status_data["seal_duel"]）：全服看得到。代打那一邊的架勢只在他的對手的卡上；不寫機率、不寫誰在線。"""
    cfg = content.config.seal
    who = side_of(duel, viewer) if viewer else None
    me = getattr(duel, who) if who else None
    active = duel.phase == "active"
    left = (duel.opened_real or now) + cfg.round_seconds - now if active else duel.starts_real - now
    return {
        "id": duel.id, "shard": content.seal[duel.shard].name if duel.shard in content.seal else duel.shard,
        "challenger": duel.challenger.name, "holder": duel.holder.name, "phase": duel.phase,
        "round": duel.round + (1 if active else 0), "rounds": duel.rounds, "edge": edge_words(duel, viewer),
        "lines": duel.lines[-3:], "sent": sent(duel), "left": max(0, int(left)), "note": CLOSE_LINE,
        "mine": who is not None,
        "tell": duel.tell.get(other(who), "") if who else "",
        "late": bool(who and active and not me.present),
        "turn": bool(who and active and me.present and who not in duel.pending),
        "gambles_left": max(0, cfg.gamble_cap - me.gambles) if me else 0,
    }


def foe_line(me: SealSide, foe: SealSide, content: Content) -> str:
    """給模型評成功率的那一行：對手是誰、身手跟你比（只寫比較，字同 battle_instance.target_line）。"""
    tuning = content.config.battle
    ratio = battle_instance.strength(tuning, foe.power) / max(1.0, battle_instance.strength(tuning, me.power))
    how = ("遠比玩家強" if ratio >= 1.5 else "比玩家強" if ratio >= 1.1 else "跟玩家不相上下" if ratio > 0.9
           else "比玩家弱" if ratio > 0.6 else "遠比玩家弱")
    hurt = "，已經帶傷" if foe.hp < foe.hp_cap * 0.5 else ""
    return f"{foe.name}（身手{how}{hurt}）"


def scene(duel: SealDuel, who: str, content: Content) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """assess_gamble 讀的 title 與 text（duel.Scene 的兩欄）。"""
    shard = content.seal[duel.shard].name if duel.shard in content.seal else duel.shard
    tell = duel.tell.get(other(who), "")
    return f"奪璽單挑・『{shard}』", f"第 {duel.round + 1}／{duel.rounds} 回合，{edge_words(duel, getattr(duel, who).name)}。{tell}"
```

### Task 8: 奪璽單挑接進 Game——開打、推進、在場、選單、暫停與收季

**Files:**
- Modify: `tianxia/engine.py`（`_seal_settle` 的單挑那一半、`_seal_challenge`、`_seal_challengers`、`_seal_spectators`、`_seal_side_of`；`_my_seal_duel`、`_seal_tick`、`_seal_options`、`_seal_act`；`options`、`choose`、`world_tick`）
- Modify: `tianxia/seal.py`（`DUEL_NOTE`、`NO_DUEL`）
- Modify: `tianxia/world.py`（`advance_world_state` 有單挑時不提前收季）
- Modify: `tianxia/sqlite_world.py`（`resume_clock` 挪單挑的時間）
- Create: `tests/test_seal_engine.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `Game._seal_tick()`、`Game._seal_options()`、`Game._seal_act(arg)`、`Game._my_seal_duel()`；選項 `seal:<招>`（三招）與灰掉的 `seal:waiting`。
- Consumes: Task 5 的 `_seal_settle`、Task 7 的 `seal_duel`。

- [ ] **Step 1: 寫失敗的測試**

Create `tests/test_seal_engine.py`：

```python
"""玉璽碎片接進 Game（玉璽碎片設計第四節～第五節；計畫 docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-12-玉璽-1-核心.md Task 8～11）。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import random

import pytest

from conftest import at
from test_seal import SALT, _ended, _registry, sealed  # noqa: F401  sealed 是夾具
from tianxia import seal, seal_duel
from tianxia.characters import CharacterStore
from tianxia.engine import Game


def _player(content, world, name, faction=None, now=0.0):
    """建一個角色、同步一次（投靠了就記進名冊）、存進資料庫（別人才讀得到他）。"""
    game = Game.new(content, name, rng=random.Random(0), world=world)
    game.state.player.faction = faction
    game.sync(now)
    CharacterStore(world.db).save(game.state)
    return game


def _stirred(content, holder_side="guan", holder="懷璽", rows=None, rounds=3):
    """長社收場、牽動了懷璽的螭首：預設懷璽（官軍，出手 3）、官甲、黃甲（戰功最高）、黃乙、黃丙（出手 1）。五個人＜10：門檻 1 回合。"""
    rows = rows or [("懷璽", "guan", 3, 0), ("官甲", "guan", 3, 1), ("黃甲", "huang", 3, 3), ("黃乙", "huang", 3, 1), ("黃丙", "huang", 1, 0)]
    return _ended("changshe_fire", rows, rounds=rounds, seal_shard="chishou", seal_holder=holder,
                  seal_holder_faction=holder_side, seal_holder_side=holder_side)


def _setup(content, world, holder="懷璽"):
    world.seed_first_season(content)
    if world.season_phase() == "preparing":
        world.open_season(content, 0.0)
    world.mutate(lambda st: setattr(st, "seal", _registry(content, chishou=holder)))


def _settle(game, content, battle, side="huang", margin="大勝"):
    game._seal_settle(battle, content.battles["changshe_fire"], {"side": side, "margin": margin})


def test_a_big_win_for_the_holder_opens_no_duel(sealed, world):
    _setup(sealed, world)
    _player(sealed, world, "懷璽", "guan")
    _settle(_player(sealed, world, "旁人"), sealed, _stirred(sealed), side="guan", margin="大勝")
    assert world.seal_state().duels == []
    assert any(r.text == seal.NO_DUEL.format(holder="懷璽", shard="螭首") for r in world.get_season().rumors)


def test_a_lost_showdown_calls_out_the_top_merit_foe(sealed, world):
    """持有者那一方大敗：從對面（黃巾）合格的人裡照戰功前三名抽；持有者親自出陣，上限 25% × 1.0 × 0.7；下戰書一句天下大事；
    不碰 Game.rng。"""
    _setup(sealed, world)
    _player(sealed, world, "懷璽", "guan")
    game = _player(sealed, world, "旁人")
    before = game.rng.getstate()
    _settle(game, sealed, _stirred(sealed))
    assert game.rng.getstate() == before
    duel = world.seal_state().duels[0]
    assert duel.challenger.name in {"黃甲", "黃乙", "黃丙"} and duel.holder.name == "懷璽"
    assert (duel.tier, duel.phase, duel.where) == ("大敗", "muster", "長社火攻") and duel.cap == pytest.approx(0.25 * 0.7)
    assert any("下了戰書" in r.text for r in world.get_season().rumors if r.layer == "world")


def test_the_snapshot_beats_a_late_defection(sealed, world):
    """4.2 第 3 條：璽動之後持有者叛到挑戰者那一方的陣營——比的是快照（guan），不是名冊；他改算旁觀、挑戰者從勝方挑、不套同陣營過濾。"""
    _setup(sealed, world)
    world.record_faction("懷璽", "huang")
    _settle(_player(sealed, world, "旁人"), sealed, _stirred(sealed))
    duel = world.seal_state().duels[0]
    assert duel.tier == "旁觀" and duel.challenger.name.startswith("黃")


def test_a_free_holder_who_enlists_with_the_winner_still_duels(sealed, world):
    """4.5：快照是散人（None）一律旁觀；他臨時投效大勝的那一方、出手滿門檻，只多護璽 ×0.7，照樣打。"""
    _setup(sealed, world)
    rows = [("懷璽", "huang", 3, 0), ("黃甲", "huang", 3, 3), ("官甲", "guan", 3, 0)]
    _settle(_player(sealed, world, "旁人"), sealed, _stirred(sealed, holder_side=None, rows=rows))
    duel = world.seal_state().duels[0]
    assert duel.tier == "旁觀" and duel.challenger.name == "黃甲" and duel.cap == pytest.approx(0.25 * 0.7)


def test_challengers_skip_turncoats_newbies_and_the_warlords(sealed, world):
    _setup(sealed, world)
    _player(sealed, world, "懷璽", "guan")
    battle = _stirred(sealed)
    battle.participants["黃甲"].turncoat = True
    battle.participants["黃乙"].newbie = True
    battle.participants["黃丙"].faction = "haoqiang"  # 站在豪強那一邊：不是交戰兩軍
    _settle(_player(sealed, world, "旁人"), sealed, battle)  # 旁人自己是新角色（新手期），也補不上
    assert world.seal_state().duels == []  # 對面無人能上前，懷璽不失
    assert any(r.text == seal.NO_DUEL.format(holder="懷璽", shard="螭首") for r in world.get_season().rumors)


def test_a_small_battle_lets_a_spectating_free_agent_step_up(sealed, world):
    """PM 癸：參戰名單不到 10 人時，人在決戰大區、剛同步過、名冊上是散人、不在新手期的旁觀者也能被挑上陣前（戰功當 0）。"""
    _setup(sealed, world)
    watcher = Game.new(sealed, "看熱鬧的", rng=random.Random(0), world=world)
    watcher.sync(0.0)
    watcher.state.player.location, watcher.state.player.joined_at = "town", -1e9  # 小鎮在北區（install_showdowns）；早就不是新手
    CharacterStore(world.db).save(watcher.state)
    game = _player(sealed, world, "旁人")
    game.now = 10.0
    _settle(game, sealed, _stirred(sealed, rows=[("懷璽", "guan", 3, 0), ("官甲", "guan", 3, 0)]), margin="險勝")
    duel = world.seal_state().duels[0]
    assert duel.challenger.name == "看熱鬧的" and duel.challenger.hp_cap > 0


def test_a_holder_unseen_this_season_fights_as_a_fresh_character_and_is_not_saved(sealed, world):
    """5.3：這一季還沒上線過（seen_real 早於 opened_real）的持有者照新角色的身手（fresh），不存回他的存檔、season_number 不被改寫。"""
    _setup(sealed, world)
    world.mutate(lambda st: setattr(st.seal, "opened_real", 1000.0))
    old = _player(sealed, world, "懷璽", "guan")
    old.state.player.seen_real = 10.0
    old.state.player.member.level = 20
    CharacterStore(world.db).save(old.state)
    _settle(_player(sealed, world, "旁人"), sealed, _stirred(sealed, holder_side=None, rows=[("黃甲", "huang", 3, 3)]))
    duel = world.seal_state().duels[0]
    assert duel.holder.fresh
    saved = CharacterStore(world.db).load("懷璽")
    assert saved.player.member.level == 20 and saved.player.season_number == old.state.player.season_number


def _open_duel(content, world, now=0.0):
    """開一場下戰書的單挑（starts_real ＝ now ＋ 180），回旁人的 Game（不是單挑的任何一方）。"""
    _setup(content, world)
    game = _player(content, world, "旁人", now=now)
    game.now = now
    _settle(game, content, _stirred(content))
    return game


def test_the_duel_runs_paced_rounds_when_nobody_is_there(sealed, world):
    """5.4：下戰書 180 秒，接著兩邊都不在場就 20 秒一回合；誰輪詢誰推，一次最多一回合；收場寫登記與天下大事。"""
    game = _open_duel(sealed, world)
    for t in (100.0, 180.0):
        with at(game, t):
            game.options()
    duel = world.seal_state().duels[0]
    assert (duel.phase, duel.round) == ("active", 0)
    t = 180.0
    while world.seal_state().duels and t < 400.0:
        t += 20.0
        with at(game, t):
            game.options()
    view = world.seal_state()
    assert view.duels == [] and view.registry.history[-1].kind in ("take", "keep")
    rounds = [r.text for r in world.get_season().rumors if r.text.startswith("奪璽第")]
    assert 1 <= len(rounds) <= 5 and len(rounds) == len(set(rounds))


def test_only_your_own_request_marks_you_present(sealed, world):
    """5.5：只有他自己的請求（own_request）記 last_seen，15 秒最多寫一次；不是自己的請求（_kin、假人、管理者建的 Game）一律不記。"""
    _open_duel(sealed, world)
    me = _player(sealed, world, world.seal_state().duels[0].challenger.name)
    with at(me, 10.0):
        me.options()
    assert world.seal_state().duels[0].challenger.last_seen is None
    me.mark_seen(20.0)
    for t, expect in ((20.0, 20.0), (30.0, 20.0), (40.0, 40.0)):
        with at(me, t):
            me.options()
        assert world.seal_state().duels[0].challenger.last_seen == expect


def test_a_present_side_gets_three_moves_and_an_absent_one_waits(sealed, world):
    """5.5：在場的那一邊選單最前面三招（寫剋哪一招）；回合開始時判成不在場的那一邊這一回合灰掉、寫中性的字（不寫代打）。"""
    _open_duel(sealed, world)
    me = _player(sealed, world, world.seal_state().duels[0].challenger.name)
    me.mark_seen(170.0)
    with at(me, 170.0):
        me.options()
    with at(me, 180.0):
        opts = me.options()
    assert [o.id for o in opts[:3]] == ["seal:強攻", "seal:固守", "seal:奇襲"] and all(o.enabled for o in opts[:3])
    holder = _player(sealed, world, "懷璽")
    holder.mark_seen(181.0)
    with at(holder, 181.0):
        late = [o for o in holder.options() if o.id.startswith("seal:")]
    assert late and not any(o.enabled for o in late) and seal_duel.LATE in late[0].label
    with at(me, 182.0):
        me.choose("seal:強攻")
    assert world.seal_state().duels[0].pending == {"challenger": "強攻"}


def test_whoever_ticks_the_duel_gets_the_same_result(sealed, tmp_path):
    """5.13：同一場單挑，由排程（Game.for_world 的 world_tick）推或由旁人的畫面推，結果一模一樣。兩個各自的資料庫。"""
    from tianxia.sqlite_world import open_world

    outcomes = []
    for i, ticker in enumerate(("world", "player")):
        world = open_world(tmp_path / f"{i}.db")
        game = _open_duel(sealed, world)
        pusher = Game.for_world(sealed, world) if ticker == "world" else game
        t = 180.0
        while world.seal_state().duels and t < 500.0:
            if ticker == "world":
                pusher.world_tick(t)
            else:
                with at(pusher, t):
                    pusher.options()
            t += 20.0
        outcomes.append(world.seal_state().registry.history[-1].model_dump())
    assert outcomes[0] == outcomes[1]


def test_a_paused_season_freezes_the_duel_and_resume_shifts_it(sealed, world):
    game = _open_duel(sealed, world)
    world.pause_clock(100.0)
    with at(game, 500.0):
        game.options()
    assert world.seal_state().duels[0].phase == "muster"
    world.resume_clock(sealed, 1100.0)  # 停了 1000 秒
    assert world.seal_state().duels[0].starts_real == pytest.approx(180.0 + 1000.0)


def test_choosing_something_else_mid_duel_does_not_roll_the_round_back(sealed, world):
    """5.12：單挑回合推進之後有人 choose() 別的動作（收尾 _save_season 整份存回賽季），回合不倒回、天下大事不重複。"""
    game = _open_duel(sealed, world)
    with at(game, 180.0):
        game.options()
    with at(game, 200.0):
        game.choose("act:rest")
    with at(game, 201.0):
        game.options()
    texts = [r.text for r in world.get_season().rumors if r.text.startswith("奪璽第")]
    assert world.seal_state().duels[0].round == len(texts) == 1


def test_the_season_does_not_end_early_while_a_duel_is_open(sealed, world, monkeypatch):
    """5.11：有沒打完的奪璽單挑時，那一個曆時不提前收季；沒有就照收。"""
    from tianxia import calendar
    from tianxia import world as world_rules

    _open_duel(sealed, world)
    monkeypatch.setattr(world_rules, "decisive_ending", lambda vehicle, content: content.scenario.endings[0])
    season = world.get_season()
    hour = calendar.cal_hour_seconds(sealed, season)
    world_rules.advance_world_state(season, sealed, hour, random.Random(0), world)
    assert not season.ended
    world.mutate(lambda st: setattr(st, "seal_duels", []))
    world_rules.advance_world_state(season, sealed, hour, random.Random(0), world)
    assert season.ended


def test_ending_the_season_leaves_the_duel_unsettled(sealed, world):
    """5.11：管理者立刻收季（自然收季同）：之後第一次推就記成未分勝負，碎片留在原主。"""
    game = _open_duel(sealed, world)
    sealed.config.admins = ["管理者"]
    _player(sealed, world, "管理者").admin_end_season(now=150.0)
    game.sync(160.0)
    with at(game, 160.0):
        game.options()
    view = world.seal_state()
    assert view.duels == [] and view.registry.history[-1].kind == "unsettled"
    assert view.registry.shards["chishou"].holder == "懷璽"
```

- 加挑人與快照：

```python
    def _seal_challenge(
        self, battle, definition: BattleDef, reg, tianji: int, winner: str, margin: str, need: int, held: set[str],
    ) -> SealDuel | None:
        """璽動那一場收場：持有者那一方打得怎樣（照璽動那一刻的快照，4.5）；不是大勝就挑挑戰者（4.6）、照 5.3 抓雙方的快照、開一場
        下戰書的單挑（還沒寫進去，呼叫端的 world.mutate 寫）。挑不出人回 None（懷璽不失、算動過）。"""
        cfg = self.content.config.seal
        holder = battle.seal_holder
        snap = battle.seal_holder_side
        moved = snap is not None and self.world.faction_of(holder) != battle.seal_holder_faction  # 璽動之後陣營變了：算旁觀、不套過濾
        side = None if moved else snap
        tier = seal.tier_for(side, winner, margin)
        if tier == "大勝" or not winner:
            return None
        armies = [f.id for f in definition.factions]
        foe = winner if side is None else next((f for f in armies if f != side), winner)
        blocked = None if side is None else battle.seal_holder_faction  # 只在快照陣營沒變時擋「自己人合演轉手」
        rows = self._seal_challengers(battle, foe, need, holder, blocked, held)
        if len(battle.participants) < cfg.small_battle:
            rows += self._seal_spectators(definition, battle, held)
        shard = reg.shards[battle.seal_shard]
        name = seal.weighted_pick([(n, m) for n, m, _ in rows], seal.dice(reg.salt, tianji, "seal-duel", battle.record_id),
                                  cfg.challenger_pool, prefer=shard.former)
        if name is None:
            return None
        challenger = next(s for n, _, s in rows if n == name)
        me = battle.participants.get(holder)
        guarded = me is not None and me.acted_rounds >= need  # 護璽：自己出手滿門檻（臨時投效的散人也算，只多這一條，4.5）
        keeper = seal_duel.side_from_participant(me, battle.seal_holder_faction) if me is not None else self._seal_side_of(holder)
        cap = min(cfg.cap, cfg.cap * cfg.side_factor.get(tier, 0.0) * (cfg.guard if guarded else 1.0))
        return seal_duel.open_duel(
            f"{battle.record_id}:{shard.id}", shard.id, battle.record_id, definition.timetable_event, definition.name, tier, cap,
            challenger, keeper, self.now, cfg,
        )

    def _seal_challengers(self, battle, side: str, need: int, holder: str, blocked: str | None, held: set[str]) -> list:
        """挑戰者的候選（4.6）：那一邊（交戰兩軍之一）的參戰者、自己出手滿 need 回合、不是持有者、手上沒有碎片、這一季沒叛投過與
        不在新手期（加入時的快照）、此刻名冊上的陣營不跟持有者的快照陣營相同（blocked；None 不擋）。回（名號, 戰功, 快照）；不看假人。"""
        tuning = self.content.config.battle
        out = []
        for p in battle.participants.values():
            if p.faction != side or p.acted_rounds < need or p.turncoat or p.newbie or p.is_bot:  # is_bot 是決戰補位的機器人（不是角色）
                continue
            if seal.key(p.name) == seal.key(holder) or seal.key(p.name) in held:
                continue
            now_faction = self.world.faction_of(p.name)
            if blocked is not None and now_faction == blocked:
                continue
            out.append((p.name, battle_instance.merit(tuning, p), seal_duel.side_from_participant(p, now_faction)))
        return out

    def _seal_spectators(self, definition: BattleDef, battle, held: set[str]) -> list:
        """小伺服器的退路（PM 癸；計畫「要 PM 定的」第 2 條）：收場那一刻人在決戰的大區、presence_seconds 內同步過、不在路上、名冊上
        是散人、不是參戰者也不是持有者、不在新手期與序章、這一季沒叛投過、手上沒有碎片的人。戰功當 0；身手照他的存檔算（不存回去）。"""
        if not hasattr(self.world, "db") or definition.region is None:
            return []
        store = CharacterStore(self.world.db)
        since = self.now - self.content.config.presence_seconds
        region = definition.region
        here = [loc for loc in self.content.locations if (r := atlas.region_of(self.content, loc)) is not None and r.id == region]
        out = []
        for loc in here:
            for state in store.present(loc, since):
                name = state.player.name
                if (name in battle.participants or seal.key(name) == seal.key(battle.seal_holder) or seal.key(name) in held
                        or state.player.journey is not None or state.player.defected or self.world.faction_of(name) is not None):
                    continue
                kin = self._kin(state)
                if roster.newbie(kin.state, self.content) or prologue_rules.active(kin.state, self.content):
                    continue
                out.append((name, 0, seal_duel.side_from(
                    name, None, kin._battle_power(), kin._battle_scores(mounted=False), kin._battle_role(), kin._battle_neili_cap(),
                )))
        return out

    def _seal_side_of(self, name: str) -> SealSide:
        """沒參戰的持有者的快照（5.3）：觸發結算的人就是他用 self；否則讀他的存檔建一份不叫模型的 Game（不 sync、不存回、不記 last_seen），
        讀不到（沒有資料庫）就照新角色。這一季沒上線過（seen_real，舊存檔退回 last_real，早於 opened_real；opened_real 是 None 一律當
        上線過）記成 fresh、身手照新角色。"""
        opened = self.world.seal_state().registry.opened_real
        game = self if seal.key(self.state.player.name) == seal.key(name) else (self._load_kin(name) or self._kin(new_game_state(self.content, name)))
        seen = game.state.player.seen_real if game.state.player.seen_real is not None else game.state.last_real
        fresh = opened is not None and (seen is None or seen < opened)
        if fresh and game is not self:
            game = self._kin(new_game_state(self.content, name))
        return seal_duel.side_from(
            name, self.world.faction_of(name), game._battle_power(), game._battle_scores(mounted=False), game._battle_role(),
            game._battle_neili_cap(), fresh=fresh,
        )
```

- 加推進、選單、出招：

```python
    def _my_seal_duel(self) -> tuple[SealDuel | None, str | None]:
        """排第一的那一場奪璽單挑，與自己是哪一邊（不在裡面是 None）。"""
        duels = self.world.seal_state().duels if seal.on(self.content) else []
        if not duels:
            return None, None
        return duels[0], seal_duel.side_of(duels[0], self.state.player.name)

    def _seal_tick(self) -> None:
        """把奪璽單挑推到現在（5.4）：options(tick=True) 與 world_tick 叫，一次最多結算一回合。自己的請求（own_request）而且自己是一方時
        順便記 last_seen（15 秒最多寫一次）。先讀一次 world 那一列，沒事做就不開 mutate（每 10 秒的輪詢不寫）。季、角色都不動；
        自己是一方就順便補給自己（_deliver_seal_results，Task 10）。"""
        if not seal.on(self.content):
            return
        view = self.world.seal_state()
        if not view.duels or view.paused:
            return
        duel = view.duels[0]
        me = self.state.player.name
        who = seal_duel.side_of(duel, me) if me else None
        cfg = self.content.config.seal
        if not seal_duel.needs_write(duel, who, self.now, cfg, own=self.own_request, ended=self.state.world.ended):
            return

        def _apply(shared) -> None:
            if self.own_request and who is not None and shared.seal_duels and shared.seal_duels[0].id == duel.id:
                seal_duel.note_seen(shared.seal_duels[0], who, self.now, cfg)
            seal_duel.advance(shared, self.content, self.now)

        self.world.mutate(_apply)

    def _seal_options(self) -> list[Option]:
        """單挑的一方、這一場開打了：三招（寫剋哪一招）擺在選單最前面；送過了換成一顆灰的「已出招」；這一回合判成不在場的三招灰掉、
        寫中性的字（5.5，不寫代打）。放手一搏走輸入框（seal_free_text_prompt，Task 9），選單上沒有。"""
        duel, who = self._my_seal_duel()
        if duel is None or who is None or duel.phase != "active":
            return []
        cfg = self.content.config.seal
        problem = seal_duel.submit_problem(duel, who, cfg)
        if problem == seal_duel.SENT:
            left = max(0, int((duel.opened_real or self.now) + cfg.round_seconds - self.now))
            label = f"（{seal_duel.SENT}：已出招 {seal_duel.sent(duel)}／2，最遲 {left // 60} 分 {left % 60} 秒）"
            return [Option(id="seal:waiting", label=label, enabled=False)]
        late = f"（{problem}）" if problem else ""
        return [Option(id=f"seal:{m}", label=duel_rules.move_label(m) + late, enabled=problem is None) for m in MOVES]

    def _seal_act(self, move: str) -> list[str]:
        """選單上的一招（seal:<招>）：在一次 world.mutate 裡重驗、記下這一手，然後推一步（湊齊了而且滿 20 秒才結算）。"""
        if move not in MOVES:
            return ["（此刻無法這麼做。）"]
        out: list[str] = []
        me, cfg = self.state.player.name, self.content.config.seal

        def _apply(shared) -> None:
            duel = shared.seal_duels[0] if shared.seal_duels else None
            who = seal_duel.side_of(duel, me) if duel is not None else None
            problem = seal_duel.GONE if who is None else seal_duel.submit_problem(duel, who, cfg)
            if problem:
                out.append(f"（{problem}）")
                return
            seal_duel.submit(duel, who, move=move)
            seal_duel.note_seen(duel, who, self.now, cfg)
            out.append(f"你出了{move}。")
            out.extend(seal_duel.advance(shared, self.content, self.now))

        self.world.mutate(_apply)
        return out
```

  （`duel_rules` 是引擎裡 `tianxia.duel` 的別名，已經有。）
- `options`：第一行 `battle_status = self._battle_for_me(tick=tick)` 之前加 `if tick: self._seal_tick()  # 奪璽單挑跟決戰一樣：誰輪詢誰推（5.4）`；集結那一支改成 `return self._seal_options() + self._battle_options(battle, definition) + self._everyday_options(odds)`，最後一支改成 `return self._seal_options() + self._everyday_options(odds)`。開打了的決戰選單那一支與暫停那一支不接（19.2 第 11 條：下一場決戰剛好同時開打時只能被代打）。
- `choose`：`if kind == "duel":` 那一段之前加：

```python
        if kind == "seal":  # 奪璽單挑：每回合的出招不寫紀錄，收場由 _deliver_seal_results 補一份戰報與一則紀錄（5.9）
            return self._log(self._seal_act(arg))
```

- `world_tick`：`self._battle_status(tick=True)` 下一行加 `self._seal_tick()`。

`tianxia/world.py` 的 `advance_world_state`：

```python
# ── Task 9：放手一搏三段式、伺服器 ──

def _active_with_me(content, world):
    """開打了、挑戰者在場（回合 1 剛開）的單挑；回挑戰者的 Game（own_request）。"""
    _open_duel(content, world)
    me = _player(content, world, world.seal_state().duels[0].challenger.name)
    me.mark_seen(170.0)
    with at(me, 170.0):
        me.options()
    with at(me, 180.0):
        me.options()
    return me


def test_the_a_step_only_reads_and_carries_the_foe_line(sealed, world):
    """5.7 A 段：鎖內、只讀；單子帶單挑 id、回合、哪一邊、文字、場景與對手的身手那一行（只寫比較，不寫數字）。"""
    me = _active_with_me(sealed, world)
    before = world.seal_state()
    with at(me, 181.0):
        request = me.seal_text_request("  踢起沙土迷他的眼  ")
    assert isinstance(request, seal_duel.TextRequest)
    assert (request.round, request.side, request.text) == (0, "challenger", "踢起沙土迷他的眼")
    assert "懷璽" in request.target and not any(ch.isdigit() for ch in request.target)
    assert world.seal_state().duels == before.duels


def test_an_absent_side_cannot_open_a_request(sealed, world):
    _active_with_me(sealed, world)
    holder = _player(sealed, world, "懷璽")
    holder.mark_seen(181.0)
    with at(holder, 181.0):
        assert holder.seal_text_request("拚了") == [f"（{seal_duel.LATE}）"]


def test_the_c_step_applies_only_to_the_same_round_side_and_text(sealed, world):
    """5.7 C 段：同一場、同一回合、同一邊、同一句才採用；回合對不上回「這一回合已經結算」、不重評；送出不等於結算。"""
    me = _active_with_me(sealed, world)
    with at(me, 181.0):
        request = me.seal_text_request("踢起沙土")
        stale = request._replace(round=request.round - 1)
        assert me.seal_gamble("踢起沙土", 70, ("甲踢起沙土。", "甲踢空了。"), request=stale) == [f"（{seal_duel.GONE}）"]
        assert me.seal_gamble("換一句", 70, ("", ""), request=request) == [f"（{seal_duel.RESEND}）"]
        me.seal_gamble("踢起沙土", 70, ("甲踢起沙土。", "甲踢空了。"), request=request)
    duel = world.seal_state().duels[0]
    assert duel.pending == {"challenger": seal_duel.TEXT} and duel.rates == {"challenger": 70} and duel.round == 0


def test_a_side_gets_no_input_box_after_two_gambles(sealed, world):
    me = _active_with_me(sealed, world)
    world.mutate(lambda st: setattr(st.seal_duels[0].challenger, "gambles", 2))
    with at(me, 181.0):
        assert me.seal_free_text_prompt() is None
        assert me.seal_text_request("再來") == [f"（{seal_duel.NO_MORE_GAMBLE}）"]


def test_the_input_box_goes_to_the_seal_duel_before_the_boss_duel(sealed, world):
    """5.7 最後一條：輸入框三選一，「正在打的決戰 > 奪璽單挑 > 頭目戰」。"""
    import server

    me = _active_with_me(sealed, world)
    with at(me, 181.0):
        view = server.main_view(me)
    assert view["free_op"] == "seal_text" and view["free_text"] == seal_duel.PROMPT


def test_everyone_sees_the_seal_card_and_the_parties_are_watched(sealed, world):
    """5.10：奪璽卡全服看得到；seal_watch 在單挑雙方與被璽動那一場的參戰者、持有者為真（網頁在背景也輪詢，5.5）。"""
    _open_duel(sealed, world)
    crowd = _player(sealed, world, "路人")
    card = crowd.status_data()["seal_duel"]
    assert card["holder"] == "懷璽" and card["shard"] == "螭首" and not card["mine"]
    assert crowd.status_data()["seal_watch"] is False
    side = _player(sealed, world, world.seal_state().duels[0].challenger.name)
    assert side.status_data()["seal_watch"] is True
```

Create `tests/test_seal_web.py`：

```python
"""奪璽的網頁那一半（玉璽碎片設計 5.5、5.10、第十節）：江湖頁的奪璽卡、送出放手一搏、背景輪詢與分頁標題、修練頁的傳承那一列。
餵真的引擎給的 /api/main 給 app.js（tests/test_prologue_web.py 的假瀏覽器）。沒有 node 就略過。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import random

import pytest
from test_prologue_web import DRIVER as PROLOGUE_DRIVER

import server
import webharness
from tianxia.engine import Game

pytestmark = pytest.mark.skipif(webharness.NODE is None, reason="沒有 node，前端畫面測試略過")

# test_prologue_web 的假瀏覽器只把它列的名字交給 H（wholeApp([...]) 寫成 `{ a, b, … }`：名單上的名字 app.js 裡一定要有，
# 不然整支載不起來）。這裡多交奪璽要直接叫的三個；碎片卡 sealHtml 只經過 pageJianghu，不必列
NAMES_START = 'wholeApp(["S", '  # 名單的開頭（結尾那幾個名字別的計畫會加，開頭比較穩）
assert PROLOGUE_DRIVER.count(NAMES_START) == 1
DRIVER = PROLOGUE_DRIVER.replace(NAMES_START, 'wholeApp(["S", "sealDuelHtml", "pollBlocked", "sealTitle", ')


def run(m, script):
    """同 test_prologue_web.run（m 是 /api/main 回的那份）；script 裡可用 H、m、T（T.ctx.document 是假瀏覽器的 document）。"""
    return webharness.run(DRIVER, {"m": m, "script": script, "S": {}, "menxia": None, "stored": {}, "responses": {}})


def _card(**over):
    return {"id": "7:chishou", "shard": "螭首", "challenger": "<b>黃甲</b>", "holder": "懷璽", "phase": "active", "round": 2,
            "rounds": 5, "edge": "黃甲略佔上風", "lines": ["奪璽第 1 回合：黃甲強攻，懷璽以固守相迎——兩邊僵持不下。"], "sent": 1,
            "left": 75, "note": "最後氣勢越偏向挑戰者，碎片越可能易主；璽有靈，未必跟著贏家走。", "mine": False, "tell": "",
            "late": False, "turn": False, "gambles_left": 0, **over}


@pytest.fixture
def m(hints_content, world):
    game = Game.new(hints_content, "沈浪", rng=random.Random(0), world=world)
    game.skip_tutorial()
    return server.main_view(game)


def test_the_jianghu_page_draws_the_seal_card_escaped(m):
    m["status"]["seal_duel"] = _card()
    page = run(m, "return H.pageJianghu();")
    assert "奪璽" in page and "螭首" in page and "第 2／5 回合" in page and "已出招 1／2" in page and "1 分 15 秒" in page
    assert "<b>黃甲</b>" not in page and "&lt;b&gt;黃甲&lt;/b&gt;" in page
    assert "璽有靈" in page


def test_the_tell_is_drawn_only_when_the_server_sends_it(m):
    m["status"]["seal_duel"] = _card(mine=True, tell="他退了半步，門戶守得嚴嚴實實。", turn=True)
    page = run(m, "return H.pageJianghu();")
    assert "他退了半步" in page and "輪到你出招" in page
    m["status"]["seal_duel"] = _card(phase="muster", round=0, left=120)
    page = run(m, "return H.pageJianghu();")
    assert "2 分 0 秒後開打" in page and "已出招" not in page


def test_the_input_box_posts_to_seal_text(m):
    m["free_text"], m["free_op"] = "寫下你陣前的一手（20 字內）", "seal_text"
    page = run(m, "return H.pageJianghu();")
    assert 'data-op="seal_text"' in page


def test_a_watched_tab_keeps_polling_in_the_background_and_flags_your_turn(m):
    """5.5：seal_watch 為真時分頁在背景也照 10 秒輪詢；輪到你出招時分頁標題改成「【奪璽】輪到你出招」，之後改回原本的標題。"""
    m["status"]["seal_watch"] = True
    m["status"]["seal_duel"] = _card(mine=True, turn=True)
    out = run(m, "T.ctx.document.hidden = true; return [H.pollBlocked(), H.sealTitle()].join('|');")
    assert out == "false|【奪璽】輪到你出招"
    m["status"]["seal_watch"] = False
    m["status"]["seal_duel"] = None
    out = run(m, "T.ctx.document.hidden = true; return [H.pollBlocked(), H.sealTitle() === H.S.baseTitle].join('|');")
    assert out == "true|true"
```

（`document` 要從 `T.ctx.document` 改：script 是在 node 外層用 `new Function` 跑的，`document` 那個名字摸不到 vm 裡的那一份。H 上的名字是 `DRIVER` 換掉的那一串給的，app.js 不必另外掛。）

`tianxia/seal_duel.py` 加（待 joy 潤）：

```python
PROMPT = "寫下你陣前的一手（20 字內）"
GAMBLE_NOTE = "放手一搏一場最多兩次；寫得越險，成了推得越多、搏輸了傷的是你自己。"
RESEND = "這一手跟剛才送出的對不上（是不是開了兩個分頁？），請再送一次。"
```

`tianxia/engine.py` 加：

```python
    def seal_free_text_prompt(self) -> str | None:
        """奪璽單挑的放手一搏輸入框（5.7）：自己是一方、這一回合在場、還沒出手、這一場還沒搏滿兩次才有。"""
        duel, who = self._my_seal_duel()
        if duel is None or who is None or seal_duel.submit_problem(duel, who, self.content.config.seal, text=True):
            return None
        return seal_duel.PROMPT

    def seal_free_text_note(self) -> str | None:
        return seal_duel.GAMBLE_NOTE if self.seal_free_text_prompt() is not None else None

    def seal_text_request(self, text: str) -> seal_duel.TextRequest | list[str]:
        """奪璽放手一搏的 A 段（鎖內、只讀，5.7）：驗證他是這場單挑的一方、這一回合開始時判他在場、還沒送出、還能搏、文字 20 字以內，
        開單子（單挑 id、回合、哪一邊、文字、名號、場景、對手那一行）。判成不在場的這一回合不開單。"""
        if self.world.paused_at() is not None:
            return [f"（{PAUSED_TEXT}。）"]
        duel, who = self._my_seal_duel()
        if duel is None or who is None:
            return ["（此刻無法這麼做。）"]
        problem = seal_duel.submit_problem(duel, who, self.content.config.seal, text=True)
        if problem:
            return [f"（{problem}）"]
        text = text.strip()[:FREE_TEXT_MAX]
        if not text:
            return ["（請先輸入你想做的事。）"]
        me, foe = getattr(duel, who), getattr(duel, seal_duel.other(who))
        title, scene_text = seal_duel.scene(duel, who, self.content)
        return seal_duel.TextRequest(
            duel=duel.id, round=duel.round, side=who, text=text, name=me.name, faction_name=self._duel_side(),
            title=title, scene=scene_text, target=seal_duel.foe_line(me, foe, self.content),
        )

    def seal_gamble(
        self, text: str, rate: int | None = None, stories: tuple[str, str] | None = None,
        request: seal_duel.TextRequest | None = None,
    ) -> list[str]:
        """奪璽放手一搏的 C 段（5.7）：鎖內重驗，同一場、同一回合、同一邊、同一句才採用，記進 pending（送出不等於結算，湊齊而且滿 20 秒才算）。
        回合對不上直接回「這一回合已經結算」，不重評、不套用（不照抄 duel_gamble 的鎖內重評：會把第 r 回合寫的那一句套到第 r＋1 回合）。
        沒給 request（腳本、測試）就在這裡開單；沒給分數就用 _quick_client 在鎖內評，評不到是 40。"""
        if request is None:
            request = self.seal_text_request(text)
            if isinstance(request, list):
                return self._log(request)
        if rate is None:
            verdict = battle_instance.assess_gamble(
                self._quick_client(), duel_rules.Scene(request.title, request.scene), request.faction_name, request.text, request.name,
                setting=seal_duel.SETTING, place=seal_duel.PLACE, target=request.target,
            )
            rate, stories = verdict.rate, (verdict.win, verdict.lose)
        checked = text.strip()[:FREE_TEXT_MAX]
        me, cfg = self.state.player.name, self.content.config.seal
        out: list[str] = []

        def _apply(shared) -> None:
            duel = shared.seal_duels[0] if shared.seal_duels else None
            if duel is None or duel.id != request.duel or duel.round != request.round or duel.phase != "active":
                out.append(f"（{seal_duel.GONE}）")
                return
            who = seal_duel.side_of(duel, me)
            if who != request.side or checked != request.text:
                out.append(f"（{seal_duel.RESEND}）")
                return
            problem = seal_duel.submit_problem(duel, who, cfg, text=True)
            if problem:
                out.append(f"（{problem}）")
                return
            seal_duel.submit(duel, who, text=checked, rate=rate, stories=stories)
            seal_duel.note_seen(duel, who, self.now, cfg)
            out.append(f"你放手一搏：「{checked}」")
            out.extend(seal_duel.advance(shared, self.content, self.now))

        self.world.mutate(_apply)
        return self._log(out)

    def seal_duel_card(self) -> dict | None:
        """江湖頁的「奪璽」卡（5.10）：排第一的那一場，全服看得到；沒有是 None。"""
        duels = self.world.seal_state().duels if seal.on(self.content) else []
        return seal_duel.card(duels[0], self.state.player.name or None, self.now, self.content) if duels else None

    def seal_watch(self) -> bool:
        """網頁在背景也要輪詢（5.5）：自己是單挑的一方；或此刻有一場被璽動、還沒收場的決戰，自己是那一片的持有者或那一場的參戰者
        （挑戰者要到收場才確定）。"""
        if not seal.on(self.content):
            return False
        _, who = self._my_seal_duel()
        if who is not None:
            return True
        status = self._battle_status(tick=False)
        if status is None or not status[0].seal_shard:
            return False
        battle, me = status[0], self.state.player.name
        return seal.key(battle.seal_holder) == seal.key(me) or me in battle.participants
```

```python
def seal_text(game: Game, text: str) -> list[str]:
    """奪璽單挑的放手一搏（玉璽碎片設計 5.7），三段同 duel_text：A Game.seal_text_request（只讀；判成不在場的回合不開單）→
    B 鎖外請模型評成功率、寫成敗兩版劇情（預算同隨口應對，評不到 40）→ C Game.seal_gamble 重驗同一場、同一回合、同一邊、同一句才用，
    回合對不上直接回「這一回合已經結算」，不在鎖內重評。"""
    started, request = _open_request(game, lambda: game.seal_text_request(text))
    if isinstance(request, list):
        return request
    total = game.content.config.free_text_budget_seconds
    fallback = battle_instance.GambleVerdict(battle_instance.DEFAULT_FREE_TEXT_SUCCESS_RATE)
    scene = duel.Scene(request.title, request.scene)

    def score():
        client = within_budget(game.client, total - (_monotonic() - started))
        if client is None and game.client is not None:
            return fallback
        return battle_instance.assess_gamble(
            client, scene, request.faction_name, request.text, request.name,
            setting=seal_duel.SETTING, place=seal_duel.PLACE, target=request.target,
        )

    verdict = model_call(game, score, fallback=fallback, left=total - (_monotonic() - started), busy=BUSY_FREE_TEXT)
    return act(game, lambda g: g.seal_gamble(request.text, verdict.rate, (verdict.win, verdict.lose), request=request))
```

- 加三個函式（H 不必在 app.js 裡掛：測試的假瀏覽器用 `wholeApp([...])` 照名字交出去，見 `tests/test_seal_web.py` 的 `DRIVER`）：

```js
  // 奪璽卡（玉璽碎片設計 5.10，status.seal_duel）：全服看得到、可以摺疊，排在懸賞卡下面（不擠 375×812 的第一屏）。
  // 雙方、哪一片、第幾回合、氣勢的字（持有者看的是倒過來的，伺服器算好）、最近兩三行、已出招幾手與倒數；代打那一邊的架勢只在對手的卡上（伺服器只給他）。
  // 字待 joy 潤
  function sealDuelHtml(d) {
    const left = `${Math.floor(d.left / 60)} 分 ${d.left % 60} 秒`;
    const when = d.phase === "muster" ? `${left}後開打` : `第 ${d.round | 0}／${d.rounds | 0} 回合・已出招 ${d.sent | 0}／2，最遲 ${left}後結算`;
    return `<details class="card seal-duel" open><summary>奪璽・『${esc(d.shard)}』</summary>
      <p><b>${esc(d.challenger)}</b> 對 懷璽的 <b>${esc(d.holder)}</b>　${esc(when)}</p>
      ${d.phase === "active" ? `<p>${esc(d.edge)}</p>` : ""}
      ${d.tell ? `<p class="tell">對手的架勢：${esc(d.tell)}</p>` : ""}
      ${d.turn ? `<p class="turn">輪到你出招。</p>` : ""}
      ${(d.lines || []).map((l) => `<p class="muted">${esc(l)}</p>`).join("")}
      <p class="muted">${esc(d.note)}</p></details>`;
  }
  // 背景分頁不輪詢；奪璽的雙方、被璽動那一場的持有者與參戰者例外（status.seal_watch，5.5），在背景也照 10 秒輪詢
  function pollBlocked() {
    const watch = !!(S.main && S.main.status && S.main.status.seal_watch);
    return pollInFlight || S.stage !== "game" || S.busy || (document.hidden && !watch);
  }
  // 輪到你出招時改分頁標題，之後改回原本的（5.5）
  function sealTitle() {
    const d = S.main && S.main.status && S.main.status.seal_duel;
    return d && d.turn ? "【奪璽】輪到你出招" : S.baseTitle;
  }
```

- `poll` 的第一行 `if (pollInFlight || S.stage !== "game" || S.busy || document.hidden) return;` 換成 `if (pollBlocked()) return;`。
- `renderTop` 裡 `el.innerHTML = html;` 那一行**之前**加 `document.title = sealTitle();`（`renderTop` 前面有兩個提早 `return`：序章換頁時交給 `render`、`#top` 不在時；放在最後的話這兩種情況標題不會改回來）。
- `pageJianghu`：`const bountyCard = …` 下一行加 `const sealCard = m.status && m.status.seal_duel && shown("orders") ? sealDuelHtml(m.status.seal_duel) : "";`，兩個 `return` 裡 `${bountyCard}` 後面都接 `${sealCard}`。
- submit 的 `form.id === "free-form"` 那一行換成 `await doMain(["duel_text", "seal_text"].includes(form.dataset.op) ? form.dataset.op : "battle_text", { text: data.text }); // 決戰、奪璽或頭目戰的放手一搏`。

```python
# ── Task 10：收場補送、碎片卡、江湖史 ──

def _finished(content, world, taken: bool, won: bool = True):
    """跑完一場單挑（管理者式的定結果：fixed＝taken）；won 時持有者的池子只剩 1（第一回合就見底，挑戰者大勝），否則挑戰者的只剩 1。
    回挑戰者的名號。兩人都還沒建角色（他們第一次同步就補送）。"""
    game = _open_duel(content, world)

    def _rig(st):
        d = st.seal_duels[0]
        d.fixed = taken
        (d.holder if won else d.challenger).hp = 1.0

    world.mutate(_rig)
    t = 180.0
    while world.seal_state().duels and t < 500.0:
        with at(game, t):
            game.options()
        t += 20.0
    event = world.seal_state().registry.history[-1]
    return event.holder if event.kind == "take" else event.other


def test_a_take_is_delivered_to_both_with_a_seal_report_and_fame_in_the_same_season(sealed, world):
    """5.9：易手只改登記；兩人各自同步時補一份戰報（kind seal，回合一行一行）與一則紀錄；失主名望 +5；補過的不補第二次；抄本照登記重抄。"""
    challenger = _finished(sealed, world, taken=True)
    old = _player(sealed, world, "懷璽", now=600.0)  # 第一次同步就補到
    report = old.state.battles[0]
    assert report.kind == "seal" and report.rounds and old.state.player.stats.get("fame", 0) == 5
    assert old.state.player.heirloom is None
    seen = old.state.player.seal_seen
    old.sync(700.0)
    assert old.state.player.seal_seen == seen and len([b for b in old.state.battles if b.kind == "seal"]) == 1
    new = _player(sealed, world, challenger, now=600.0)
    assert new.state.battles[0].kind == "seal" and new.state.player.heirloom.template == "chishou"


def test_a_near_miss_gives_the_challenger_one_fame_and_the_holder_three(sealed, world):
    """PM 丁：挑戰者打贏單挑（大勝）、再擲沒中，名望 +1；持有者懷璽不失 +3。"""
    challenger = _finished(sealed, world, taken=False, won=True)
    holder = _player(sealed, world, "懷璽", now=600.0)
    them = _player(sealed, world, challenger, now=600.0)
    assert holder.state.player.stats.get("fame", 0) == 3 and them.state.player.stats.get("fame", 0) == 1


def test_a_holder_back_only_next_season_gets_the_report_but_no_fame(sealed, world):
    """5.9：跨季才回來的只補戰報與紀錄（名望每季重來），抄本照登記。"""
    from conftest import next_season

    _finished(sealed, world, taken=True)
    old = Game.new(sealed, "懷璽", rng=random.Random(0), world=world)  # 建好但這一季沒同步過
    next_season(sealed, world, old)
    assert any(b.kind == "seal" for b in old.state.battles) and old.state.player.stats.get("fame", 0) == 0


def test_the_emergence_winner_learns_the_emerge_skill_in_the_same_season(sealed, world):
    """11.2 第 4 條：混元一氣改給現世的得主，只在補送當下還是同一季、還沒學過、持有沒滿才給。測試內容沒有混元一氣，借一門內容武學當 emerge_skill。"""
    from test_seal import _ended
    from tianxia import library

    skill = next(s for s in sealed.skills if s not in sealed.config.starter_skills)
    sealed.config.seal.emerge_skill = skill
    _setup(sealed, world)
    game = _player(sealed, world, "旁人")
    winner = _player(sealed, world, "得主", "guan")
    game._seal_settle(_ended("wancheng_jia", [("得主", "guan", 9, 9)], seal_emerge="yunzhuan"), sealed.battles["wancheng_jia"],
                      {"side": "guan", "margin": "大勝"})
    winner.sync(50.0)
    assert winner.state.player.heirloom.template == "yunzhuan" and skill in library.owned_arts(winner.state)
    assert any("雲篆" in e.tag for e in winner.state.journal)


def test_the_holder_card_tells_the_shard_its_effect_and_the_risky_dates(sealed, world):
    """第十節：持有者的碎片卡寫哪一片與效果、「新得之季，此季不動」或「此季已動過」、這一季標了 seal 還沒到的決戰時刻（Game.stamp）；不寫機率。"""
    _setup(sealed, world)
    holder = _player(sealed, world, "懷璽")
    card = holder.status_data()["seal"]
    assert card["name"] == "螭首" and "氣血上限" in card["effect_text"] and len(card["dates"]) == 2
    assert not any("%" in d for d in card["dates"]) and _player(sealed, world, "路人").status_data()["seal"] is None


def test_the_chronicle_opens_with_the_seven_shards(sealed, world):
    _setup(sealed, world)
    text = _player(sealed, world, "路人").chronicle_text()
    head = text.split("---")[0]
    assert "傳國玉璽" in head and "螭首" in head and "懷璽" in head and head.count("未現世") == 6
```

`tests/test_seal.py` 檔尾加：

```python
# ── Task 10：隱沒 ──

def test_a_shard_sinks_only_after_three_absent_seasons_and_twenty_eight_days(sealed):
    """PM 庚：連續三季沒上線、而且距上次上線滿 28 天才隱沒；上線了就歸零；opened_real 是 None 不動；查不到那一列（None）照沉。"""
    from tianxia import seal

    cfg = sealed.config.seal
    day = 86400.0
    reg = _registry(sealed, chishou="甲", xuanniu="乙")
    reg.opened_real = 100 * day
    for season in (2, 3):
        assert seal.sink(reg, season, {"甲": 50 * day, "乙": 101 * day}, 110 * day, cfg) == []
    assert (reg.shards["chishou"].absent, reg.shards["xuanniu"].absent) == (2, 0)
    sunk = seal.sink(reg, 4, {"甲": 50 * day, "乙": 101 * day}, 110 * day, cfg)
    assert [(s.id, name) for s, name in sunk] == [("chishou", "甲")] and reg.shards["chishou"].state == "sunk"
    assert reg.shards["chishou"].holder is None and reg.history[-1].kind == "sink"
    reg2 = _registry(sealed, chishou="甲")
    reg2.opened_real = 100 * day
    reg2.shards["chishou"].absent = 2
    assert seal.sink(reg2, 4, {"甲": 99 * day}, 110 * day, cfg) == []  # 三季沒上線，但距上次上線還不到 28 天
    reg2.opened_real = None
    assert seal.sink(reg2, 5, {"甲": None}, 300 * day, cfg) == [] and reg2.shards["chishou"].absent == 3  # 判斷不了：不動


def test_next_season_sinks_absent_holders_and_clears_the_duels(sealed, world):
    """7.6、5.11：換季那一筆交易裡判隱沒（江湖史只寫「不知所蹤」）、清空 seal_duels（還沒收場的記成未分勝負）、寫新的 opened_real。"""
    from conftest import next_season
    from tianxia import seal
    from tianxia.engine import Game
    from test_seal_duel import _duel

    world.seed_first_season(sealed)
    if world.season_phase() == "preparing":
        world.open_season(sealed, 0.0)
    reg = _registry(sealed, chishou="不見人")
    reg.opened_real = 0.0
    reg.shards["chishou"].absent = 2
    world.mutate(lambda st: (setattr(st, "seal", reg), st.seal_duels.append(_duel())))
    game = Game.new(sealed, "路人", rng=random.Random(0), world=world)
    next_season(sealed, world, game)  # 「不見人」那一列不存在：當成沒上線、而且滿 28 天
    view = world.seal_state()
    assert view.registry.shards["chishou"].state == "sunk" and view.duels == []
    assert {"sink", "unsettled"} <= {e.kind for e in view.registry.history}
    assert any("不知所蹤" in line for _, entries in world.chronicle_before(view.season) for line in [r.text for r in entries])
```

`tests/test_seal_engine.py` 再加一支假人：

```python
def test_a_retired_bot_holding_a_shard_may_come_back_regardless_of_gaps(sealed, world):
    """7.6：每季開季後第一次補人，持碎片的退隱假人各擲一次回不回來（bot_return_chance），不看缺額；回來的派到名冊人數最少的陣營。"""
    from tianxia.bot_runner import BotRunner, TickReport
    from tianxia.state import BotProfile

    _setup(sealed, world, holder="老假人")
    bot = Game.new(sealed, "老假人", rng=random.Random(0), world=world)
    bot.state.player.bot = BotProfile(personality="普通", seed=1, faction="guan", season_number=0)  # 上一季的：退隱
    CharacterStore(world.db).save(bot.state)
    sealed.config.seal.bot_return_chance = 1.0
    runner = BotRunner(sealed, world, CharacterStore(world.db), random.Random(0), clock=lambda: 100.0)
    report = TickReport()
    runner._wake_seal_bots(100.0, report)
    woken = CharacterStore(world.db).load("老假人")
    assert woken.player.bot.season_number == world.seal_state().season and report.added == 1
    runner._wake_seal_bots(110.0, report)
    assert report.added == 1  # 這一季擲過了
```

（`BotProfile` 的欄位照 `state.py` 實際的寫；`personality` 要是 `server_bots` 認得的值。）
